```json
  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "krivapeta-c01",
      "source_id": "krivapeta-s1",
      "quote": "La Krivapeta è una figura mitologica delle tradizioni popolari delle Valli del Natisone.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Krivapeta adalah tokoh mitologis dalam tradisi rakyat Valli del Natisone.",
        "en": "The Krivapeta is a mythological figure of the folk traditions of the Valli del Natisone."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "krivapeta-c02",
      "source_id": "krivapeta-s1",
      "quote": "Essa non è una strega, ma viene rappresentata come una donna che vive isolata dalla gente, abita in grotte o anfratti, vicino ai torrenti, ed ha la particolarità di avere i piedi ritorti (con il tallone all'avanti e le dita dietro)",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia bukan penyihir, melainkan perempuan yang hidup terasing di gua atau celah batu dekat sungai, dengan kaki terbalik: tumit di depan dan jari di belakang.",
        "en": "She is not a witch but a woman living apart in caves or crevices near streams, with reversed feet: heels in front and toes behind."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "krivapeta-c03",
      "source_id": "krivapeta-s1",
      "quote": "difetto da cui prende origine il nome di Krivapeta (dallo sloveno kriv = curvo, ritorto e peta = tallone)",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Namanya berasal dari cacat itu: bahasa Slovenia kriv, \"bengkok\", dan peta, \"tumit\".",
        "en": "Her name comes from this defect: Slovene kriv, \"crooked\", and peta, \"heel\"."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "krivapeta-c04",
      "source_id": "krivapeta-s1",
      "quote": "Le Krivapete vengono descritte come donne dai capelli verdi che ricoprono loro le spalle e vestite di bianco.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Para Krivapeta digambarkan sebagai perempuan berambut hijau hingga menutupi bahu dan berpakaian putih.",
        "en": "The Krivapete are described as women with green hair covering their shoulders, dressed in white."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "krivapeta-c05",
      "source_id": "krivapeta-s1",
      "quote": "Le Krivapete possono essere cattive con gli uomini oppure buone e pronte a dare loro consigli su come realizzare cose ancora ignote ed a suggerire ai contadini i momenti più adatti alla semina",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Mereka bisa jahat kepada manusia, atau baik dan memberi nasihat, misalnya memberi tahu petani waktu terbaik untuk menabur.",
        "en": "They can be cruel to people, or kind and ready to advise them, telling farmers the best time to sow."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "krivapeta-c06",
      "source_id": "krivapeta-s1",
      "quote": "In cambio dell'aiuto però possono richiedere di portare via i bambini piccoli in quanto alcune sono crudeli e cannibali.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Sebagai imbalan bantuannya mereka bisa meminta membawa pergi anak kecil, karena sebagian dari mereka kejam dan kanibal.",
        "en": "In return for their help they may demand to take away small children, since some are cruel and cannibalistic."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "krivapeta-c07",
      "source_id": "krivapeta-s1",
      "quote": "I racconti dei nonni, con la loro atmosfera paurosa, servivano quindi, in tempi in cui i giovani giocavano per lo più nei prati e nei boschi, a far sì che i piccoli si tenessero lontani dai luoghi pericolosi.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Cerita kakek-nenek yang menakutkan ini berfungsi menjauhkan anak-anak dari tempat berbahaya seperti gua, jurang, dan sungai.",
        "en": "The grandparents' frightening tales served to keep children away from dangerous places such as caves, cliffs and streams."
      }
    }
  ],
```

```json
  "short_description": {
    "id": "La Serena adalah makhluk setengah perempuan setengah burung dari bestiari Katalan dan legenda Gandesa yang memikat lelaki dengan harpa.",
    "en": "La Serena is a half-woman, half-bird being of Catalan bestiaries and a Gandesa legend who lures men with her harp.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "la-serena-c01",
      "la-serena-c02",
      "la-serena-c04"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "La Serena muncul dalam bestiari Katalan abad ke-14 hingga ke-16 dan dalam legenda Gandesa di pegunungan Pàndols dan Cavalls. Menurut legenda, seorang gadis cantik berubah menjadi makhluk setengah perempuan setengah burung karena patah hati. Ia berambut panjang kemerahan, berparuh bengkok, bercakar, bersayap cokelat, dan menyerang ternak para gembala.",
      "en": "La Serena appears in Catalan bestiaries of the 14th to 16th centuries and in a Gandesa legend set in the Pàndols and Cavalls ranges. In the legend a beautiful girl turned into a half-woman, half-bird being after a disappointment in love. She has long reddish hair, a hooked beak, talons and brown wings, and attacked the shepherds' flocks.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "la-serena-c01",
        "la-serena-c02",
        "la-serena-c03",
        "la-serena-c06"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Pada malam bulan purnama ia memainkan harpa untuk memikat lelaki, yang jatuh tertidur dan tak pernah bangun lagi. Dahulu kisah ini dipakai menakuti anak nakal.",
      "en": "On full-moon nights she played the harp to lure men, who fell asleep and never woke. In the past the tale was used to frighten naughty children.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "la-serena-c04",
        "la-serena-c05",
        "la-serena-c07"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Mateu Aubà menyelamatkan legenda ini dari tuturan perempuan tua di Gandesa, dan buku anak karya Àngels Cid menghidupkannya kembali.",
      "en": "Mateu Aubà rescued the legend from the telling of old women in Gandesa, and a children's book by Àngels Cid revived it.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "la-serena-c08",
        "la-serena-c09"
      ]
    }
  ],

      "id": "la-serena-s1",
      "url": "https://ca.wikipedia.org/wiki/La_Serena_(mitologia)",
      "title": "La Serena (mitologia)",
      "author": null,
      "publisher": "Wikipedia (ca)",
      "published": null,
      "language": "ca",

    }
  ],
  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "la-serena-c01",
      "source_id": "la-serena-s1",
      "quote": "La Serena és un personatge mitològic present en diversos bestiaris catalans i en una coneguda llegenda de Gandesa ambientada a les serres de Pàndols i Cavalls (Terra Alta).",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "La Serena adalah tokoh mitologis dalam beberapa bestiari Katalan dan legenda terkenal dari Gandesa yang berlatar pegunungan Pàndols dan Cavalls (Terra Alta).",
        "en": "La Serena is a mythological figure found in several Catalan bestiaries and in a well-known legend of Gandesa set in the Pàndols and Cavalls ranges (Terra Alta)."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "la-serena-c02",
      "source_id": "la-serena-s1",
      "quote": "La llegenda narra com una jove molt bella es transformà en un ésser híbrid –meitat dona, meitat ocell– a causa d'un desengany amorós.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Legenda itu menceritakan seorang gadis cantik yang berubah menjadi makhluk setengah perempuan setengah burung karena patah hati.",
        "en": "The legend tells how a beautiful girl became a hybrid being, half woman and half bird, because of a disappointment in love."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "la-serena-c03",
      "source_id": "la-serena-s1",
      "quote": "Amb una llarga cabellera rogenca, un bec petit i corbat, unes garres de moixó i dues ales de plomatge marró, se la descriu com una depredadora, una bèstia ferotge que atacava els ramats dels pastors.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Berambut panjang kemerahan, berparuh kecil bengkok, bercakar burung, dan bersayap cokelat, ia digambarkan sebagai pemangsa buas yang menyerang ternak para gembala.",
        "en": "With long reddish hair, a small hooked beak, bird talons and two brown-feathered wings, she is described as a fierce predator that attacked the shepherds' flocks."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "la-serena-c04",
      "source_id": "la-serena-s1",
      "quote": "Però també se la coneix perquè tocava l'arpa per atraure els homes a les nits de lluna plena.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia juga dikenal memainkan harpa untuk memikat lelaki pada malam bulan purnama.",
        "en": "She is also known for playing the harp to lure men on full-moon nights."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "la-serena-c05",
      "source_id": "la-serena-s1",
      "quote": "Enganyats pel so dolç de l'instrument, s'acostaven cap a ella enamorats per la música i queien en un estat de somnolència del qual no despertaven mai més.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Terpikat suara harpa yang merdu, para lelaki mendekat lalu jatuh tertidur dan tidak pernah bangun lagi.",
        "en": "Lured by the sweet sound, the men approached her and fell into a slumber from which they never woke."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "la-serena-c06",
      "source_id": "la-serena-s1",
      "quote": "El personatge de la Serena apareix descrit en els bestiaris catalans que es conserven actualment, datats entre els segles XIV i XVI.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Tokoh Serena digambarkan dalam bestiari Katalan yang masih tersimpan, berasal dari abad ke-14 hingga ke-16.",
        "en": "The Serena is described in the surviving Catalan bestiaries, dated between the 14th and 16th centuries."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "la-serena-c07",
      "source_id": "la-serena-s1",
      "quote": "antigament s'explicava en l'àmbit familiar per espantar els xiquets, a qui s'amenaçava dient-los que, si no es portaven bé, se'ls enduria la bèstia.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dahulu legenda ini diceritakan di rumah untuk menakuti anak-anak: bila tidak berkelakuan baik, makhluk itu akan membawa mereka.",
        "en": "In the past it was told at home to frighten children, who were warned the beast would take them if they misbehaved."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "la-serena-c08",
      "source_id": "la-serena-s1",
      "quote": "Fou el gandesà Mateu Aubà qui s'interessà a recuperar-la, davant la possibilitat que pogués perdre's. Unes dones grans del poble li la transmeteren.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Mateu Aubà dari Gandesa berusaha menyelamatkan legenda ini agar tidak hilang, dan beberapa perempuan tua di desa menuturkannya kepadanya.",
        "en": "Mateu Aubà of Gandesa set out to recover the legend before it was lost, and some old women of the town passed it on to him."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "la-serena-c09",
      "source_id": "la-serena-s1",
      "quote": "El 2013, a iniciativa de Mateu Aubà i finançament de l'ajuntament de Gandesa, es va publicar un conte infantil a càrrec de la il·lustradora Àngels Cid, el qual va servir per a recuperar definitivament la llegenda de la Serena.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pada 2013, atas prakarsa Mateu Aubà dan dana pemerintah kota Gandesa, terbit buku cerita anak karya ilustrator Àngels Cid yang menghidupkan kembali legenda Serena.",
        "en": "In 2013, on Mateu Aubà's initiative and with Gandesa town council funding, a children's book by the illustrator Àngels Cid was published that finally revived the legend of the Serena."
      }
    }
  ],
  "conflicts": [],

}
```

```json
    {
      "id": "Lampago atau lympago adalah hewan heraldik mitos berwujud manusia-harimau atau manusia-singa, bertubuh harimau atau singa dan berkepala manusia. Ia dibedakan dari manticore dan satyral yang mirip. Pemakaiannya yang paling terkenal adalah pada lambang keluarga Radford dari Plymstock, Devon, yang memuat tiga lampago perak di atas dasar hitam.",
      "en": "The lampago or lympago is a mythical heraldic beast, a man-tiger or man-lion with a tiger's or lion's body and a man's head. It is distinguished from the similar manticore and satyral.   "short_description": {
```
```json
    "id": "Laurieddhu adalah peri rumah usil dari Italia selatan, lelaki kecil bertopi runcing yang duduk di dada orang tidur dan membalas kebaikan dengan emas.",
    "en": "The Laurieddhu is a mischievous household sprite of southern Italy, a little man in a pointed hat who sits on sleepers' chests and repays kindness with gold.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "laurieddhu-c01",
      "laurieddhu-c03",
      "laurieddhu-c04",
      "laurieddhu-c05"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Laurieddhu atau Scazzamurrieddhru adalah peri usil dalam cerita rakyat Italia selatan, dikenal dengan banyak nama, misalnya Munaciello di Napoli. Ia lelaki kecil jelek berbulu seukuran anak tiga tahun, berpakaian warna tembakau dan bertopi runcing. Meski kecil, ia sangat berat dan suka duduk di dada orang tidur.",
      "en": "The Laurieddhu or Scazzamurrieddhru is a mischievous sprite of the folklore of southern Italy, known by many names, such as Munaciello in Naples. It is an ugly, hairy little man the size of a three-year-old, in tobacco-coloured clothes and a pointed hat. Though small, it is very heavy and likes to sit on sleepers' chests.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "laurieddhu-c01",
        "laurieddhu-c02",
        "laurieddhu-c03",
        "laurieddhu-c04"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Orang mengambil hatinya dengan memberi sepatu, dan ia membalas dengan koin emas; ia juga dapat diperas dengan mencuri topinya. Ia mengepang surai kuda dengan kuat, tetapi baik kepada gadis yang ditindas ibu tiri dan bahkan mengerjakan pekerjaan rumah mereka.",
      "en": "People win its favour with a gift of shoes, and it repays them with gold; it can also be blackmailed by stealing its hat. It braids horses' manes tightly, but is kind to girls oppressed by stepmothers, even doing their housework.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "laurieddhu-c05",
        "laurieddhu-c06",
        "laurieddhu-c07",
        "laurieddhu-c08"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Mitos ini dirunut ke roh incubus dalam tulisan Petronius dan Plinius serta ke lares dan penates. Domenico Modugno membuat lagu tentangnya pada 1954.",
      "en": "The myth is traced to the incubus spirit in Petronius and Pliny and to the lares and penates. Domenico Modugno wrote a song about it in 1954.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "laurieddhu-c09",
        "laurieddhu-c10"
      ]
    }
  ],
  "cultural_context": null,
  "etymology": null,
  "story_mode": {

  "tradition_vs_modern": null,
  "learning_questions": [],

  "sources": [
    {
      "id": "laurieddhu-s1",
      "url": "https://it.wikipedia.org/wiki/Laurieddhu",
      "title": "Laurieddhu",
      "author": null,
      "publisher": "Wikipedia (it)",
      "published": null,
      "language": "it",
      "type": "wikipedia",
      "accessed": "2026-10-02"
    }
  ],
  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "laurieddhu-c01",
      "source_id": "laurieddhu-s1",
      "quote": "Il Laurieddhu o Scazzamurrieddhru è un dispettoso folletto del folklore dell'Italia meridionale. Il nome \"Laurieddhu\" viene utilizzato in particolare nell'area ionico-salentina.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Laurieddhu atau Scazzamurrieddhru adalah peri usil dalam cerita rakyat Italia selatan; nama Laurieddhu dipakai terutama di daerah Ionia-Salento.",
        "en": "The Laurieddhu or Scazzamurrieddhru is a mischievous sprite of the folklore of southern Italy; the name Laurieddhu is used especially in the Ionian-Salento area."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "laurieddhu-c02",
      "source_id": "laurieddhu-s1",
      "quote": "A Barletta è conosciuto come Schezzamurid, ad Andria come Scazzamrridd. In Daunia è noto come Scazzamurill, a Napoli come Munaciello",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Di Barletta ia disebut Schezzamurid, di Andria Scazzamrridd, di Daunia Scazzamurill, dan di Napoli Munaciello.",
        "en": "In Barletta it is called Schezzamurid, in Andria Scazzamrridd, in Daunia Scazzamurill and in Naples Munaciello."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "laurieddhu-c03",
      "source_id": "laurieddhu-s1",
      "quote": "È descritto sia come poco più che un bambino o anche come un omino brutto e peloso, delle dimensioni di un bambino di circa tre anni, con un abito color tabacco, spesso scalzo e con un cappello a punta in testa",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia digambarkan seperti anak kecil atau lelaki kecil jelek berbulu seukuran anak tiga tahun, berpakaian warna tembakau, sering tanpa alas kaki, dan bertopi runcing.",
        "en": "It is described as little more than a child, or an ugly hairy little man the size of a three-year-old, in tobacco-coloured clothes, often barefoot and wearing a pointed hat."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "laurieddhu-c04",
      "source_id": "laurieddhu-s1",
      "quote": "Nonostante le piccole dimensioni sarebbe assai forte e pesantissimo, e ha l'abitudine di sedersi sulla pancia o sul petto di chi dorme, guastandogli il sonno",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Meski kecil, ia sangat kuat dan berat, dan suka duduk di perut atau dada orang tidur hingga merusak tidurnya.",
        "en": "Though small, it is very strong and heavy and likes to sit on sleepers' bellies or chests, spoiling their sleep."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "laurieddhu-c05",
      "source_id": "laurieddhu-s1",
      "quote": "Per ingraziarselo gli si possono donare un paio di scarpe, o si possono lasciare dei sassolini nelle proprie pantofole la notte; lo Scazzamurrieddhru ripaga queste gentilezze con monete d'oro",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Untuk mengambil hatinya orang memberinya sepasang sepatu atau meninggalkan kerikil di sandal pada malam hari, dan ia membalas dengan koin emas.",
        "en": "To win its favour people give it a pair of shoes or leave pebbles in their slippers at night, and it repays the kindness with gold coins."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "laurieddhu-c06",
      "source_id": "laurieddhu-s1",
      "quote": "Lo si può invece ricattare rubandogli il cappello.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia dapat diperas dengan mencuri topinya.",
        "en": "It can be blackmailed by stealing its hat."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "laurieddhu-c07",
      "source_id": "laurieddhu-s1",
      "quote": "talvolta li cura e li nutre, talaltra li tormenta (per esempio annodando le criniere e le code dei cavalli, creando trecce così resistenti che si diceva che l'unico pettine efficace fosse il crocifisso)",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Terhadap hewan peliharaan, kadang ia merawat dan memberi makan, kadang menyiksa, misalnya mengepang surai dan ekor kuda begitu kuat sehingga konon hanya salib yang bisa mengurainya.",
        "en": "With domestic animals it sometimes tends and feeds them, sometimes torments them, braiding horses' manes and tails so tightly that only a crucifix was said to untangle them."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "laurieddhu-c08",
      "source_id": "laurieddhu-s1",
      "quote": "È benevolo con le fanciulle, e le protegge dalle angherie delle matrigne e delle padrone, arrivando a fare i lavori di casa al posto loro.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia baik kepada gadis-gadis, melindungi mereka dari ibu tiri dan majikan yang kejam, bahkan mengerjakan pekerjaan rumah mereka.",
        "en": "It is kind to girls, protecting them from cruel stepmothers and mistresses, even doing their housework for them."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "laurieddhu-c09",
      "source_id": "laurieddhu-s1",
      "quote": "Il cantautore Domenico Modugno nel 1954 gli ha dedicato una canzone, chiamandolo con il nome usato nel Brindisino cioè Scarcagnulu",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Penyanyi-penulis lagu Domenico Modugno pada 1954 membuat lagu tentangnya dengan nama yang dipakai di daerah Brindisi, Scarcagnulu.",
        "en": "In 1954 the singer-songwriter Domenico Modugno dedicated a song to it, using the Brindisi name Scarcagnulu."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "laurieddhu-c10",
      "source_id": "laurieddhu-s1",
      "quote": "L'origine del mito dello Scazzamurrieddhru si può far risalire a quello dello spirito incubo citato da Petronio Arbitro, Plinio, Sant'Agostino e altri, ma anche, per altri aspetti, alle figure dei lari  (da cui il nome laurieddhu) e dei penati.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Asal mitos ini dapat dirunut ke roh incubus yang disebut Petronius Arbiter, Plinius, dan Santo Agustinus, serta ke lares (sumber nama laurieddhu) dan penates.",
        "en": "The myth can be traced to the incubus spirit mentioned by Petronius Arbiter, Pliny and Saint Augustine, and also to the lares (source of the name laurieddhu) and penates."
      }
    }
  ],
  "conflicts": [],
  "gaps": [
    {
      "field": "sources",
      "searched": "Hanya tersedia artikel Wikipedia it untuk makhluk ini; belum ditemukan sumber kedua yang dapat dikutip."
    },
    {
      "field": "images",
      "searched": "Gambar Commons belum dicari pada putaran ini; dijadwalkan pada pemeriksaan gambar terpisah."
    }
  ]
}
```

```json
  "short_description": {
    "id": "Laùro adalah peri rumah dari kepercayaan rakyat Salento, mirip Brownie, yang membantu petani atau mengganggu orang tidur.",
    "en": "The laùro is a household sprite of Salento folk belief, like the brownie, that helps farmers or troubles sleepers.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "lauro-c01",
      "lauro-c02",
      "lauro-c05",
      "lauro-c06"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Laùro adalah makhluk dari kepercayaan rakyat Salento, semacam peri kecil yang sangat mirip Brownie Britania. Konon mereka roh anak-anak yang belum dibaptis. Kehadirannya di rumah ditandai suara langkah di atap pada malam hari atau bunyi genderang dan bola memantul.",
      "en": "The laùro is a being of Salento folk belief, a kind of sprite very like the British brownie. They were said to be the spirits of unbaptised children. Their presence in a house is revealed by footsteps on the roof at night or the sound of drums and bouncing balls.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "lauro-c01",
        "lauro-c02",
        "lauro-c03",
        "lauro-c04"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Lauri yang baik membantu petani, misalnya menjaga hewan pada malam hari. Lauri yang jahat duduk di dada orang tidur hingga sesak napas, atau mengepang surai kuda hingga harus dipotong agar kuda itu tidak mati.",
      "en": "Benign lauri help farmers, for example tending animals at night. Malign ones sit on sleepers' chests and take their breath away, or braid horses' manes so tightly they must be cut or the horse dies.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "lauro-c05",
        "lauro-c06",
        "lauro-c07"
      ]
    }
  ],
  "cultural_context": null,

    }
  ],
  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "lauro-c01",
      "source_id": "lauro-s1",
      "quote": "Il laùro (più spesso llaurieddru o raùlo) è un essere di una radicatissima credenza popolare salentina.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Laùro (lebih sering llaurieddru atau raùlo) adalah makhluk dari kepercayaan rakyat Salento yang sangat mengakar.",
        "en": "The laùro (more often llaurieddru or raùlo) is a being of a deeply rooted folk belief of Salento."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "lauro-c02",
      "source_id": "lauro-s1",
      "quote": "I lauri sono una specie di folletti, molto simili ai Brownie britannici, che spesso in passato sono stati avvistati da una miriade di persone.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Para lauri adalah semacam peri kecil yang sangat mirip Brownie Britania, yang dahulu konon sering dilihat banyak orang.",
        "en": "The lauri are a kind of sprite very like the British brownies, said in the past to have been seen by countless people."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "lauro-c03",
      "source_id": "lauro-s1",
      "quote": "Si dice che fossero spiriti di bambini non battezzati.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Konon mereka adalah roh anak-anak yang belum dibaptis.",
        "en": "They were said to be the spirits of unbaptised children."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "lauro-c04",
      "source_id": "lauro-s1",
      "quote": "L'avvistamento di un lauro in casa può avvenire quando la notte si sentono passi sul tetto, o rumore di tamburi o di palle che rimbalzano.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kehadiran lauro di rumah ditandai suara langkah di atap pada malam hari, atau bunyi genderang atau bola memantul.",
        "en": "A lauro in the house is revealed by footsteps on the roof at night, or the sound of drums or bouncing balls."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "lauro-c05",
      "source_id": "lauro-s1",
      "quote": "Se si trattava di lauri benigni, aiutavano i contadini nei lavori più disparati, ad esempio badando agli animali durante la notte",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Lauri yang baik membantu petani dalam berbagai pekerjaan, misalnya menjaga hewan pada malam hari.",
        "en": "Benign lauri helped farmers with all sorts of work, such as tending the animals at night."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "lauro-c06",
      "source_id": "lauro-s1",
      "quote": "Possono tuttavia essere anche maligni, arrivando in alcuni casi a sedersi sul petto delle persone durante il sonno facendo mancare loro il respiro",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Namun mereka juga bisa jahat, misalnya duduk di dada orang yang sedang tidur hingga sesak napas.",
        "en": "They can also be malign, sitting on people's chests while they sleep and taking their breath away."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "lauro-c07",
      "source_id": "lauro-s1",
      "quote": "in altri casi fanno trecce indistricabili ai cavalli: l'unica soluzione è allora tagliare la criniera, altrimenti il cavallo muore durante la notte.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kadang mereka mengepang surai kuda hingga tak dapat diurai, dan satu-satunya jalan adalah memotong surainya, kalau tidak kuda itu mati pada malam hari.",
        "en": "Sometimes they braid horses' manes inextricably, and the only remedy is to cut the mane, or the horse dies in the night."
      }
    }
  ],
```

```json
  "short_description": {
    "id": "Lenghelo adalah roh rumah usil tetapi tidak jahat dari Castelli Romani, tinggi dan ramping, yang melompat di perut orang tidur.",
    "en": "The lenghelo is a mischievous but harmless household spirit of the Castelli Romani, tall and lanky, that jumps on sleepers' bellies.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "lenghelo-c01",
      "lenghelo-c02",
      "lenghelo-c03",
      "lenghelo-c04"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Lenghelo adalah peri dalam tradisi rakyat Castelli Romani. Ia tinggi dan ramping, sehingga dinamai lenghelo, \"panjang\". Roh usil tetapi tidak jahat ini berjalan di tangga kayu atau bersembunyi di bawahnya, dan melompat di perut orang yang tidak menghormati keluarganya saat mereka tidur.",
      "en": "The lenghelo is a sprite of the folk tradition of the Castelli Romani. It is tall and lanky, hence its name, meaning \"long\". This mischievous but not wicked spirit walks on wooden stairs or hides beneath them, and jumps on the bellies of people who disrespect their families while they sleep.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "lenghelo-c01",
        "lenghelo-c02",
        "lenghelo-c03",
        "lenghelo-c04"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Ia menyembunyikan atau memecahkan benda kecil, tetapi juga dapat membuat orang menemukan uang. Konon setiap keluarga punya satu lenghelo, dan ia muncul dalam ungkapan dialek Marino tentang menari di atas perut.",
      "en": "It hides or breaks small objects but can also make people find money. Every family was said to have one, and it appears in a Marino dialect saying about dancing on someone's belly.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "lenghelo-c05",
        "lenghelo-c06",
        "lenghelo-c07"
      ]
    }
  ],
  "cultural_context": null,

  ],
  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "lenghelo-c01",
      "source_id": "lenghelo-s1",
      "quote": "Il lenghelo, detto anche lenghero, lenghelu o familiarmente lengheletto, è un folletto o spiritello presente nella tradizione popolare dei Castelli Romani.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Lenghelo, disebut juga lenghero, lenghelu, atau lengheletto, adalah peri atau roh kecil dalam tradisi rakyat Castelli Romani.",
        "en": "The lenghelo, also called lenghero, lenghelu or affectionately lengheletto, is a sprite in the folk tradition of the Castelli Romani."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "lenghelo-c02",
      "source_id": "lenghelo-s1",
      "quote": "Secondo la leggenda ha un aspetto alto e longilineo, da cui il nome lenghelo, cioè \"lungo\" o \"allungato\".",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut legenda ia tinggi dan ramping, sehingga dinamai lenghelo, yang berarti \"panjang\".",
        "en": "According to legend it is tall and lanky, hence the name lenghelo, meaning \"long\" or \"elongated\"."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "lenghelo-c03",
      "source_id": "lenghelo-s1",
      "quote": "Spiritello dispettoso ma non malvagio, secondo la tradizione popolare si può osservare in varie situazioni: cammina sulle scale di legno, oppure si nasconde nei sottoscala.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Roh usil tetapi tidak jahat ini dapat terlihat berjalan di tangga kayu atau bersembunyi di bawah tangga.",
        "en": "A mischievous but not wicked spirit, it can be seen walking on wooden stairs or hiding beneath them."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "lenghelo-c04",
      "source_id": "lenghelo-s1",
      "quote": "Disturba con scherzi coloro che non rispettano i propri familiari o semplicemente le persone a lui antipatiche letteralmente saltando loro sulla pancia durante il sonno.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia mengganggu orang yang tidak menghormati keluarganya atau yang tidak disukainya dengan melompat di perut mereka saat tidur.",
        "en": "It pranks those who disrespect their families, or simply people it dislikes, by jumping on their bellies while they sleep."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "lenghelo-c05",
      "source_id": "lenghelo-s1",
      "quote": "Inoltre, nasconde o rompe piccoli oggetti nella casa, ma può anche far trovare soldi",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia menyembunyikan atau memecahkan benda kecil di rumah, tetapi juga dapat membuat orang menemukan uang.",
        "en": "It hides or breaks small objects in the house, but can also make people find money."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "lenghelo-c06",
      "source_id": "lenghelo-s1",
      "quote": "Secondo una tradizione popolare, il rifugio del lenghelo sarebbe all'interno di Palazzo Sforza-Cesarini di Genzano di Roma, ma la credenza più diffusa, almeno nel passato, è che ogni famiglia ne avesse uno",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Satu tradisi menyebut persembunyiannya di Palazzo Sforza-Cesarini di Genzano di Roma, tetapi kepercayaan yang lebih umum adalah setiap keluarga punya satu lenghelo.",
        "en": "One tradition puts its refuge in the Palazzo Sforza-Cesarini in Genzano di Roma, but the more common belief was that every family had one."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "lenghelo-c07",
      "source_id": "lenghelo-s1",
      "quote": "Il lenghelo compare nella tipica espressione in dialetto marinese Te ballo sopp'a panza comm'u lenghelu!",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Lenghelo muncul dalam ungkapan dialek Marino \"Te ballo sopp'a panza comm'u lenghelu!\" (\"Aku menari di perutmu seperti lenghelo!\").",
        "en": "The lenghelo appears in the Marino dialect saying \"Te ballo sopp'a panza comm'u lenghelu!\" (\"I'll dance on your belly like the lenghelo!\")."
      }
    }
  ],
```

```json
  "short_description": {
    "id": "Llufa adalah makhluk perempuan halus dari cerita rakyat Empordà yang di sebagian tempat dianggap penegak keadilan.",
    "en": "The llufa is an ethereal female being of the folklore of the Empordà, seen in some places as a bringer of justice.",
    "claim_ids": [

      "llufa-c02"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Llufa adalah makhluk perempuan yang halus dan tak berwujud jelas dalam budaya rakyat Empordà. Di Roses dan Cap de Creus mereka bermaksud baik dan menegakkan keadilan, sedangkan di Baix Empordà mereka lebih mirip peri kecil yang disebut \"encantades angin dan asap\".",
      "en": "The llufa is an ethereal, indeterminate female being of the folk culture of the Empordà. In Roses and Cap de Creus they are well-meaning and just, while in the Baix Empordà they are more like little sprites called \"encantades of wind and smoke\".",

        "llufa-c02"
      ]

        "en": "In Catalan mythology a llufa is a female, ethereal, indeterminate being of the folk culture of the Empordà."

    {

      "source_id": "llufa-s1",
      "quote": "A Roses i el Cap de Creus, les llufes són benintencionades i justicieres, mentre que al Baix Empordà són més aviat una mena de petits follets que es coneixen també com a «encantades de vent i de fum».",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Di Roses dan Cap de Creus para llufa bermaksud baik dan menegakkan keadilan, sedangkan di Baix Empordà mereka semacam peri kecil yang juga disebut \"encantades angin dan asap\".",
        "en": "In Roses and Cap de Creus the llufes are well-meaning and just, while in the Baix Empordà they are more like little sprites also called \"encantades of wind and smoke\"."
      }
```

```json
    "id": "Loberno adalah makhluk mitologi Galisia hasil perkawinan serigala dan rubah, bertatapan melumpuhkan dan memakan mayat di kuburan.",
    "en": "The loberno is a Galician mythological creature born of a wolf and a vixen, with a paralysing gaze, that eats the dead in cemeteries.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "loberno-c01",
      "loberno-c04"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Loberno adalah makhluk mitologi Galisia, hasil perkawinan serigala dan rubah betina. Kadang ia mirip rubah, kadang mirip serigala berwarna jingga. Ia tuli dan tidak lari bila diteriaki, sehingga orang harus kabur dan meninggalkan barang bawaannya untuk dimakan. Tatapannya dapat melumpuhkan, ia dapat melihat menembus dinding, dan ia menggali mayat di kuburan untuk dimakan.",
      "en": "The loberno is a creature of Galician mythology, born of a wolf and a vixen. Sometimes it looks like a fox, sometimes like an orange wolf. It is deaf and does not flee when shouted at, so one must run and leave one's belongings for it to eat. Its gaze can paralyse, it can see through walls, and it digs up the dead in cemeteries to eat them.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "loberno-c01",
        "loberno-c02",
        "loberno-c03",
        "loberno-c04"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Martín Sarmiento menempatkannya di sekitar Santo André de Teixido dan menyebutnya lebih buruk dari serigala karena mengisap darah ternak. Kamus Xoán Cuveiro Piñol abad ke-19 mengartikan loberno sebagai lynx.",
      "en": "Martín Sarmiento placed it around Santo André de Teixido and called it worse than the wolf because it sucks cattle's blood. Xoán Cuveiro Piñol's 19th-century dictionary defined loberno as a lynx.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "loberno-c05",
        "loberno-c06",
        "loberno-c07"
      ]

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "loberno-c01",
      "source_id": "loberno-s1",
      "quote": "O loberno é un ser mitolóxico da cultura galega, froito da unión entre un lobo e unha raposa.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Loberno adalah makhluk mitologis budaya Galisia, hasil perkawinan serigala dan rubah betina.",
        "en": "The loberno is a mythological being of Galician culture, the offspring of a wolf and a vixen."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "loberno-c02",
      "source_id": "loberno-s1",
      "quote": "Nalgunhas ocasión semella moito máis un raposo e noutras un lobo de cor alaranxada ou con manchas desta cor.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kadang ia lebih mirip rubah, kadang mirip serigala berwarna jingga atau bertotol jingga.",
        "en": "Sometimes it looks more like a fox, other times like an orange wolf or one with orange patches."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "loberno-c03",
      "source_id": "loberno-s1",
      "quote": "Ao atoparse diante del é inútil berrarlle porque é xordo e non escapa. O único que se pode facer é fuxir deixando atrás as propias pertenzas das que se alimentará.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Berteriak kepadanya sia-sia karena ia tuli dan tidak lari; satu-satunya jalan adalah kabur dengan meninggalkan barang bawaan, yang akan dimakannya.",
        "en": "Shouting at it is useless because it is deaf and does not flee; the only thing to do is run, leaving behind one's belongings, which it will eat."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "loberno-c04",
      "source_id": "loberno-s1",
      "quote": "Fascínate coa mirada e mesmo pode paralizarte ademais de poder ver a través das paredes. Cóase nos cemiterios para desenterrar mortos e comelos.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Tatapannya memukau dan bahkan bisa melumpuhkan, ia dapat melihat menembus dinding, dan menyelinap ke kuburan untuk menggali dan memakan mayat.",
        "en": "Its gaze fascinates and can even paralyse, it can see through walls, and it slips into cemeteries to dig up and eat the dead."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "loberno-c05",
      "source_id": "loberno-s1",
      "quote": "Martín Sarmiento no seu Catálogo de voces y frases de la lengua gallega sitúao na zona de Santo André de Teixido e arredores.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Martín Sarmiento dalam Catálogo de voces y frases de la lengua gallega menempatkannya di sekitar Santo André de Teixido.",
        "en": "Martín Sarmiento, in his Catálogo de voces y frases de la lengua gallega, places it around Santo André de Teixido."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "loberno-c06",
      "source_id": "loberno-s1",
      "quote": "Descríbeo como un animal pernicioso, peor co lobo pois só succiona o sangue do ganado facendo moito mal.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Sarmiento menggambarkannya sebagai hewan berbahaya, lebih buruk dari serigala karena hanya mengisap darah ternak dan sangat merugikan.",
        "en": "Sarmiento describes it as a harmful animal, worse than the wolf because it only sucks the cattle's blood, doing great harm."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "loberno-c07",
      "source_id": "loberno-s1",
      "quote": "Xoán Cuveiro Piñol no seu dicionario do século XIX describía a voz loberno como un lince",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Xoán Cuveiro Piñol dalam kamus abad ke-19 mengartikan kata loberno sebagai lynx.",
        "en": "Xoán Cuveiro Piñol, in his 19th-century dictionary, defined the word loberno as a lynx."
```

```json
  "short_description": {
    "id": "Lobisomem adalah manusia serigala dalam cerita rakyat Brasil; anak lelaki ketujuh dapat menjadi lobisomem dan berubah pada malam bulan purnama.",
    "en": "The lobisomem is the werewolf of the folklore of Brazil; a seventh son may become one and transform on full-moon nights.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "lobisomem-c01",
      "lobisomem-c03",
      "lobisomem-c05"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Lobisomem adalah makhluk dalam cerita rakyat Amerika Selatan yang di Brasil biasanya dianggap manusia serigala. Dalam beberapa versi ia berupa bola api atau babi besar. Dalam wujud manusia ia tampak biasa kecuali telinga agak runcing dan kulit pucat kekuningan. Namanya berasal dari kata Portugis lobo, \"serigala\", dan homem, \"manusia\".",
      "en": "The lobisomem is a creature of South American folklore, in Brazil usually thought of as a werewolf. In some versions it is a ball of fire or a huge pig. In human form it looks normal except for slightly pointed ears and pale yellowish skin. Its name comes from Portuguese lobo, \"wolf\", and homem, \"man\".",
      "claim_ids": [
        "lobisomem-c01",
        "lobisomem-c02",
        "lobisomem-c07"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Anak lelaki ketujuh dapat menjadi lobisomem kecuali seorang kakaknya menjadi ayah baptisnya; anak lelaki pertama setelah tujuh anak perempuan berubah pertama kali pada usia tiga belas tahun. Ia berubah pada malam bulan purnama, menurut sebagian orang hanya pada hari Jumat, dan menyerang pejalan serta hewan di jalan.",
      "en": "A seventh son can become a lobisomem unless an elder brother becomes his godfather; the first son after seven daughters first transforms at thirteen. It transforms on full-moon nights, some say only on Fridays, and attacks travellers and animals on the road.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "lobisomem-c03",
        "lobisomem-c04",
        "lobisomem-c05",
        "lobisomem-c06"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Di sebagian Amazon dipakai sebutan Cumacanga, yang mungkin versi perempuan lobisomem.",
      "en": "In parts of the Amazon the term Cumacanga is used, possibly for a female lobisomem.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "lobisomem-c08"
      ]
    }
  ],

  ],
  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "lobisomem-c01",
      "source_id": "lobisomem-s1",
      "quote": "The lobisomem is a creature in the folklore of South America.  In Brazil, it is usually thought to be a werewolf, and \"lobisomem\" is the usual Portuguese word for it.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Lobisomem adalah makhluk dalam cerita rakyat Amerika Selatan; di Brasil ia biasanya dianggap manusia serigala, dan lobisomem adalah kata Portugis yang lazim untuknya.",
        "en": "The lobisomem is a creature of South American folklore; in Brazil it is usually thought of as a werewolf, and lobisomem is the usual Portuguese word for one."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "lobisomem-c02",
      "source_id": "lobisomem-s1",
      "quote": "In some versions, however, he is depicted as a ball of fire, or an unusually large pig. In human form, it is said to look like a normal person except for slightly pointed ears and yellowish pale skin.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam beberapa versi ia berupa bola api atau babi yang sangat besar; dalam wujud manusia ia tampak biasa kecuali telinga agak runcing dan kulit pucat kekuningan.",
        "en": "In some versions it is a ball of fire or an unusually large pig; in human form it looks normal except for slightly pointed ears and pale yellowish skin."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "lobisomem-c03",
      "source_id": "lobisomem-s1",
      "quote": "Being a family's seventh son, in which case the son can be saved by making one of his elder brothers his godfather",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Anak lelaki ketujuh sebuah keluarga menjadi lobisomem, tetapi dapat diselamatkan bila salah satu kakaknya menjadi ayah baptisnya.",
        "en": "A family's seventh son becomes a lobisomem, but can be saved if one of his elder brothers becomes his godfather."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "lobisomem-c04",
      "source_id": "lobisomem-s1",
      "quote": "Being the first male son of a couple with seven daughters. In which case the first transformation takes place on the said son's thirteenth birthday",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Anak lelaki pertama setelah tujuh anak perempuan juga menjadi lobisomem, dan perubahan pertamanya terjadi pada ulang tahun ketiga belas.",
        "en": "The first son of a couple with seven daughters also becomes one, first transforming on his thirteenth birthday."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "lobisomem-c05",
      "source_id": "lobisomem-s1",
      "quote": "Some say that a lobisomem transforms every day in which the moon is in its full phase, others say that the change only occurs on Fridays with such condition.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Sebagian orang berkata lobisomem berubah setiap bulan purnama, sebagian lagi hanya pada hari Jumat saat bulan purnama.",
        "en": "Some say a lobisomem transforms every day of the full moon, others only on Fridays under a full moon."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "lobisomem-c06",
      "source_id": "lobisomem-s1",
      "quote": "Either way, in all versions it transforms at night, usually attacking travelers and animals on the road.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam semua versi ia berubah pada malam hari dan biasanya menyerang pejalan dan hewan di jalan.",
        "en": "In all versions it transforms at night, usually attacking travellers and animals on the road."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "lobisomem-c07",
      "source_id": "lobisomem-s1",
      "quote": "The name is derived from Latin lupus + homo, respectively lobo (\"wolf\"), and homem (\"man\") in Portuguese.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Namanya berasal dari bahasa Latin lupus dan homo, dalam bahasa Portugis lobo (\"serigala\") dan homem (\"manusia\").",
        "en": "The name comes from Latin lupus and homo, Portuguese lobo (\"wolf\") and homem (\"man\")."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "lobisomem-c08",
      "source_id": "lobisomem-s1",
      "quote": "In some parts of the Amazon the term Cumacanga is used, though it is unclear if this is the female version of lobisomem, or if this is a different but related story.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Di sebagian Amazon dipakai sebutan Cumacanga, tetapi tidak jelas apakah itu versi perempuan lobisomem atau kisah lain yang berkaitan.",
        "en": "In parts of the Amazon the term Cumacanga is used, though it is unclear whether it is a female lobisomem or a different, related story."
      }
    }
  ],
  "conflicts": [],
```

```json
  "short_description": {
    "id": "Lobo branco adalah serigala putih dalam mitologi Galisia, pertanda kematian, atau menurut versi lain raja serigala yang melindungi manusia.",
    "en": "The lobo branco is a white wolf of Galician mythology, an omen of death, or in another version the king of wolves who protects people.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "lobo-branco-c01",
      "lobo-branco-c04",
      "lobo-branco-c05"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Lobo branco adalah penampakan dalam mitologi Galisia yang dianggap pertanda kematian. Di Limia ia serigala putih besar yang melolong lebih keras dari kawanannya untuk mengabarkan kematian seorang warga. Di Vila de Cruces, seekor serigala putih yang lolos dari perburuan konon masih melolong setiap malam.",
      "en": "The lobo branco is an apparition of Galician mythology taken as an omen of death. In the Limia it is a large white wolf that howls louder than its pack to announce a local's death. In Vila de Cruces a white wolf that escaped a hunt is said to howl every night.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "lobo-branco-c01",
        "lobo-branco-c02",
        "lobo-branco-c03"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Versi Xosé Fernández Ferreiro sangat berbeda: serigala putih adalah raja tunggal semua serigala dan sahabat manusia, yang menuntut serigala lain menghormati manusia dan diam-diam mengawal orang pulang. Ia hidup seratus tahun tanpa pasangan karena serigala betina takut kepadanya.",
      "en": "Xosé Fernández Ferreiro's version is very different: the white wolf is the sole king of all wolves and a friend of humans, requiring other wolves to respect people and secretly escorting travellers home. It lives a hundred years unmated, because she-wolves fear it.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "lobo-branco-c04",
        "lobo-branco-c05",
        "lobo-branco-c06",
        "lobo-branco-c07"
      ]
    }

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "lobo-branco-c01",
      "source_id": "lobo-branco-s1",
      "quote": "O lobo branco é unha das aparicións descritas na mitoloxía galega, cuxa presenza se interpreta como un agoiro de morte, o que o asemella a outros seres míticos como a raposa do aire ou a cadela peregrina.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Lobo branco (serigala putih) adalah salah satu penampakan dalam mitologi Galisia yang dianggap pertanda kematian, mirip raposa do aire dan cadela peregrina.",
        "en": "The lobo branco (white wolf) is one of the apparitions of Galician mythology, taken as an omen of death, like the raposa do aire or the cadela peregrina."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "lobo-branco-c02",
      "source_id": "lobo-branco-s1",
      "quote": "Na Limia descríbese como un lobo grande, de cor branca, que oubea máis forte que os outros lobos da manda, anunciando a morte dun veciño do lugar.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Di Limia ia digambarkan sebagai serigala besar berwarna putih yang melolong lebih keras dari serigala lain dalam kawanannya, mengabarkan kematian seorang warga.",
        "en": "In the Limia it is described as a large white wolf that howls louder than the rest of the pack, announcing the death of a local."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "lobo-branco-c03",
      "source_id": "lobo-branco-s1",
      "quote": "Os veciños montaron unha partida de caza e conseguiron matalos, menos a un, un gran lobo branco que segue ouveando alí tódalas noites.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Di Vila de Cruces, warga memburu kawanan serigala dan membunuh semuanya kecuali seekor serigala putih besar yang masih melolong di sana setiap malam.",
        "en": "In Vila de Cruces the villagers hunted a wolf pack and killed all but one, a great white wolf that still howls there every night."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "lobo-branco-c04",
      "source_id": "lobo-branco-s1",
      "quote": "Segundo el, o lobo branco era amigo dos homes e esixía nos demais lobos, ós que dominaba e dirixía, que respectasen ó home, para que o home respectase ós lobos.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut Xosé Fernández Ferreiro, serigala putih adalah sahabat manusia yang memimpin serigala lain dan menuntut mereka menghormati manusia agar manusia menghormati serigala.",
        "en": "According to Xosé Fernández Ferreiro, the white wolf was a friend of humans who ruled the other wolves and required them to respect people, so that people would respect wolves."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "lobo-branco-c05",
      "source_id": "lobo-branco-s1",
      "quote": "Só existía un único lobo branco (porque de aparecer outro, tiñan que loitar e un dos dous debía morrer), que era o rei de tódolos lobos do mundo",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Hanya ada satu serigala putih, raja semua serigala di dunia; bila muncul yang lain, keduanya harus bertarung sampai salah satu mati.",
        "en": "There was only one white wolf, king of all the wolves in the world; if another appeared they had to fight until one died."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "lobo-branco-c06",
      "source_id": "lobo-branco-s1",
      "quote": "Para evitar que os lobos atacasen a un home, acompañábao no camiño á súa casa como protección, pero sempre sen deixarse ver",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Untuk mencegah serigala menyerang seseorang, ia menemani orang itu pulang sebagai pelindung tanpa pernah terlihat.",
        "en": "To keep wolves from attacking a man it escorted him home as protection, always unseen."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "lobo-branco-c07",
      "source_id": "lobo-branco-s1",
      "quote": "O lobo branco vivía cen anos, sempre só e sen aparearse, pois as lobas lle temían e lle fuxían.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Serigala putih hidup seratus tahun, selalu sendirian dan tanpa pasangan, karena serigala betina takut dan lari darinya.",
        "en": "The white wolf lived a hundred years, always alone and unmated, because she-wolves feared and fled it."
      }
    }
  ],
```

```json
  "short_description": {
    "id": "Loogaroo adalah pengubah wujud pengisap darah dalam cerita rakyat Karibia, sering penyihir yang melepas kulitnya menjadi bola api pada malam hari.",
    "en": "The Loogaroo is a bloodsucking shapeshifter of Caribbean folklore, often a witch who sheds her skin to become a fireball at night.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "loogaroo-c01",

  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Di Karibia dan diaspora Afrika, Loogaroo adalah sejenis pengubah wujud, penyihir, atau vampir yang mirip manusia serigala; namanya dari kata Prancis loup-garou dan ia berkaitan dengan Soucouyant. Dalam sebagian mitos ia penyihir perempuan yang menjual jiwanya kepada Iblis dan harus memberinya darah setiap malam. Ia melepas kulitnya di bawah pohon kapuk lalu menjadi bola cahaya biru yang mencari darah.",
      "en": "Across the Caribbean and the African diaspora, the Loogaroo is a kind of shapeshifter, sorcerer or vampire similar to a werewolf; its name comes from French loup-garou and it is related to the Soucouyant. In some myths she is a witch who sold her soul to the Devil and must give him blood every night. She sheds her skin under a silk cotton tree and becomes a blue ball of light seeking blood.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "loogaroo-c01",
        "loogaroo-c02",
        "loogaroo-c03",
        "loogaroo-c04",
        "loogaroo-c05"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Dalam versi yang lebih baru ia lelaki kaya yang bersekutu dengan Iblis. Di Haiti, lougawou berubah menjadi hewan dan mengisap darah orang tidur. Di Trinidad dan Tobago, Lagahoo tampak sebagai lelaki berpeti mati di leher dengan tiga lilin, dan harus dipukul dengan tongkat yang dibasahi air suci selama sembilan hari. Di Mauritius, Lougarou muncul saat bulan purnama.",
      "en": "In later versions he is a wealthy man in league with the Devil. In Haiti the lougawou turns into an animal and drains the blood of sleepers. In Trinidad and Tobago the Lagahoo appears as a man with a coffin on his neck topped by three candles, and must be struck with a holy-water stick for nine days. In Mauritius the Lougarou appears at full moon.",

    {

      "source_id": "loogaroo-s1",
      "quote": "Across the Caribbean and African Disapora, the Loogaroo, Ligaroo, Lougarou or Lagahoo is a type of shapeshifter, sorcerer or vampire, similar to a werewolf.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Di Karibia dan diaspora Afrika, Loogaroo, Ligaroo, Lougarou, atau Lagahoo adalah sejenis pengubah wujud, penyihir, atau vampir yang mirip manusia serigala.",
        "en": "Across the Caribbean and the African diaspora, the Loogaroo, Ligaroo, Lougarou or Lagahoo is a kind of shapeshifter, sorcerer or vampire similar to a werewolf."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "loogaroo-c02",
      "source_id": "loogaroo-s1",
      "quote": "The term comes from the French word for werewolf, loup-garou. It is related to the myth of the Soucouyant.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Namanya berasal dari kata Prancis untuk manusia serigala, loup-garou, dan berkaitan dengan mitos Soucouyant.",
        "en": "The term comes from the French word for werewolf, loup-garou, and is related to the myth of the Soucouyant."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "loogaroo-c03",
      "source_id": "loogaroo-s1",
      "quote": "In some myths, like the Soucouyant, the Loogaroo is described as a witch who has sold her soul to the Devil. She is able to shed her skin to turn into a vampiric fireball, known as a \"corpse candle\".",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam sebagian mitos, seperti Soucouyant, Loogaroo adalah penyihir perempuan yang menjual jiwanya kepada Iblis dan dapat melepas kulitnya untuk menjadi bola api pengisap darah yang disebut \"corpse candle\".",
        "en": "In some myths, like the Soucouyant, the Loogaroo is a witch who sold her soul to the Devil and can shed her skin to become a vampiric fireball called a \"corpse candle\"."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "loogaroo-c04",
      "source_id": "loogaroo-s1",
      "quote": "According to these myths, she will have magical abilities only if she gives the Devil blood every night.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut mitos ini, ia hanya punya kekuatan sihir bila memberi Iblis darah setiap malam.",
        "en": "According to these myths she keeps her magic only if she gives the Devil blood every night."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "loogaroo-c05",
      "source_id": "loogaroo-s1",
      "quote": "The Loogahoo can leave her own skin (usually under a \"Devil Tree,\" a silk cotton tree) and turn into a flame or blue ball of bright light that haunts the night searching for blood",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia meninggalkan kulitnya, biasanya di bawah \"pohon Iblis\" yaitu pohon kapuk, lalu menjadi nyala api atau bola cahaya biru yang berkeliaran malam hari mencari darah.",
        "en": "She leaves her skin, usually under a \"Devil Tree\", a silk cotton tree, and becomes a flame or bright blue ball of light that roams the night seeking blood."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "loogaroo-c06",
      "source_id": "loogaroo-s1",
      "quote": "In later adaptations of the myth, Loogahoo is a man that has been corrupted by wealth and has dealt with the Devil to keep his status.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam adaptasi yang lebih baru, Loogahoo adalah lelaki yang dirusak kekayaan dan bersekutu dengan Iblis untuk mempertahankan kedudukannya.",
        "en": "In later versions Loogahoo is a man corrupted by wealth who has made a pact with the Devil to keep his status."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "loogaroo-c07",
      "source_id": "loogaroo-s1",
      "quote": "In Haiti, the lougawou is a human, usually female, who transforms into an animal and drains blood from sleeping victims.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Di Haiti, lougawou adalah manusia, biasanya perempuan, yang berubah menjadi hewan dan mengisap darah korban yang tidur.",
        "en": "In Haiti the lougawou is a human, usually female, who turns into an animal and drains blood from sleeping victims."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "loogaroo-c08",
      "source_id": "loogaroo-s1",
      "quote": "At night, he is described as taking the appearance of a man with a coffin on his neck instead of a head, atop which are three lit candles.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Di Trinidad dan Tobago, Lagahoo pada malam hari tampak sebagai lelaki yang lehernya memikul peti mati alih-alih kepala, dengan tiga lilin menyala di atasnya.",
        "en": "In Trinidad and Tobago the Lagahoo appears at night as a man with a coffin on his neck instead of a head, with three lit candles on top."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "loogaroo-c09",
      "source_id": "loogaroo-s1",
      "quote": "To defeat him, he must be repeatedly struck with a stick doused in holy water and holy oils for nine days.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Untuk mengalahkannya, ia harus dipukul berulang kali dengan tongkat yang dibasahi air suci dan minyak suci selama sembilan hari.",
        "en": "To defeat him he must be struck repeatedly with a stick soaked in holy water and holy oils for nine days."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "loogaroo-c10",
      "source_id": "loogaroo-s1",
      "quote": "In Mauritian folklore, the Lougarou is a notorious figure mostly used to scare children. It appears at full moon and brings trouble to the local population.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam cerita rakyat Mauritius, Lougarou adalah tokoh terkenal yang dipakai menakuti anak-anak; ia muncul saat bulan purnama dan membawa masalah bagi penduduk.",
        "en": "In Mauritian folklore the Lougarou is a notorious figure used mostly to scare children; it appears at full moon and brings trouble to the locals."
      }
    }
```

```json
    "id": "Mâchecroute adalah naga legendaris sungai Rhône di Lyon yang menyebabkan banjir dan diarak sebagai boneka pada karnaval Mardi Gras.",
    "en": "The Mâchecroute is a legendary dragon of the Rhône at Lyon that caused floods and was paraded as a puppet at the Mardi Gras carnival.",

    ]

  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Mâchecroute adalah naga legendaris yang hidup di sungai Rhône di Lyon, dekat lokasi jembatan Guillotière sekarang. Ia konon menyebabkan banjir dan kekeringan serta memangsa manusia dan hewan. Pada karnaval Mardi Gras, bonekanya diarak lalu dilempar ke Rhône; Rabelais menggambarkannya bermata besar, berkepala raksasa, dan berahang penuh gigi.",
      "en": "The Mâchecroute is a legendary dragon living in the Rhône at Lyon, near today's Guillotière bridge. It was said to cause floods and droughts and to devour people and animals. At the Mardi Gras carnival its puppet was paraded and thrown into the Rhône; Rabelais describes it with huge eyes, a giant head and toothy jaws.",

Annie Cazenave melihatnya sebagai variasi tema naga Kristen, dan Jacques Béthemont membandingkannya dengan Tarasque.",
      "en": "The legend arose in the Middle Ages, was mentioned by Rabelais in 1552 and faded after the Rhône was embanked in 1856. In Isère and Savoie it was also a child-eating bogeyman.       "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Rabelais menggambarkan boneka karnavalnya bermata lebih besar dari perutnya, berkepala lebih besar dari seluruh tubuhnya, dan berahang lebar mengerikan penuh gigi.",
        "en": "Rabelais describes the carnival puppet with eyes bigger than its belly, a head bigger than the rest of its body, and wide, horrific, well-toothed jaws."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "machecroute-c05",
      "source_id": "machecroute-s1",
      "quote": "La légende de la Mâchecroute est apparue au Moyen âge. Elle est évoquée par Rabelais dans Le Quart-Livre en 1552.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Legenda Mâchecroute muncul pada Abad Pertengahan dan disebut Rabelais dalam Le Quart-Livre pada 1552.",
        "en": "The Mâchecroute legend arose in the Middle Ages and is mentioned by Rabelais in Le Quart-Livre in 1552."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "machecroute-c06",
      "source_id": "machecroute-s1",
      "quote": "La légende aurait perdu en vitalité avec l'endiguement des rives du Rhône en 1856, qui a permis de réguler crues et inondations.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Legenda ini memudar setelah tepian Rhône ditanggul pada 1856, yang mengendalikan banjir.",
        "en": "The legend faded after the Rhône's banks were embanked in 1856, which controlled floods."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "machecroute-c07",
      "source_id": "machecroute-s1",
      "quote": "Selon Annie Cazenave, ces mythes sont des variations « populaires » sur la thématique chrétienne du dragon, ou encore du Léviathan.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut Annie Cazenave, mitos-mitos ini adalah variasi rakyat atas tema naga Kristen atau Leviathan.",
        "en": "According to Annie Cazenave these myths are popular variations on the Christian theme of the dragon, or of Leviathan."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "machecroute-c08",
      "source_id": "machecroute-s1",
      "quote": "la Mâchecroute, peut également être présentée comme un « croquemitaine », aquatique ou non, dévorant préférentiellement les enfants.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut penelitian Charles Joisten di Isère dan Savoie, Mâchecroute juga dapat berupa momok, di air atau di darat, yang terutama memangsa anak-anak.",
        "en": "According to Charles Joisten's fieldwork in Isère and Savoie, the Mâchecroute could also be a bogeyman, aquatic or not, that preferably devoured children."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "machecroute-c09",
      "source_id": "machecroute-s1",
      "quote": "Le géographe Jacques Béthemont rapproche la Mâchecroute de la Tarasque de Tarascon ou de la Gargouille de Rouen.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ahli geografi Jacques Béthemont membandingkan Mâchecroute dengan Tarasque dari Tarascon atau Gargouille dari Rouen.",
        "en": "The geographer Jacques Béthemont compares the Mâchecroute with the Tarasque of Tarascon or the Gargouille of Rouen."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "machecroute-c10",
      "source_id": "machecroute-s1",
      "quote": "Le festival Entre Rhône et Saône l'a choisie comme mascotte en 2022.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Festival Entre Rhône et Saône memilihnya sebagai maskot pada 2022.",
        "en": "The Entre Rhône et Saône festival chose it as its mascot in 2022."
      }
    }
  ],
  "conflicts": [],
  "gaps": [
```

```json
    "id": "Marfanto adalah arwah gentayangan dari Terres de l'Ebre yang menjerit di rawa pada malam tanpa bulan.",
    "en": "The marfanto is a lost soul of the Terres de l'Ebre that shrieks in the marshes on moonless nights.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "marfanto-c01",
      "marfanto-c02"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Marfanto adalah semacam arwah gentayangan dari Baix Ebre dan Montsià. Mereka makhluk alam kubur yang menderita, berkeliaran di rawa pada malam tanpa bulan dan menjerit hingga darah orang membeku; jeritan itu dianggap personifikasi angin setempat. Ada dugaan mereka kenangan akan budak, buronan, atau penyelundup yang dahulu berkeliaran di delta Ebro.",
      "en": "The marfanto is a kind of lost soul of the Baix Ebre and Montsià. They are grieving beings from beyond the grave who roam the marshes on moonless nights, shrieking blood-chilling cries thought to personify the local wind. Some suggest they are a memory of slaves, fugitives or smugglers who once roamed the Ebro delta.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "marfanto-c01",
        "marfanto-c02",
        "marfanto-c03",
        "marfanto-c04"
      ]
    },

      "id": "Versi perempuannya, marfanta, dipakai menakuti anak nakal. Kamus DCVB mengartikan marfanta sebagai hantu atau orang yang menyamar pada malam hari untuk menakuti.",
      "en": "Its female form, the marfanta, was used to scare naughty children. The DCVB dictionary defines marfanta as a ghost or a person disguised at night to frighten.",

  ],
  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "marfanto-c01",
      "source_id": "marfanto-s1",
      "quote": "Un marfanto és, dins la mitologia catalana, una mena d'ànima en pena pròpia de les Terres de l'Ebre, concretament, de les comarques del Baix Ebre i el Montsià.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam mitologi Katalan, marfanto adalah semacam arwah gentayangan dari Terres de l'Ebre, khususnya Baix Ebre dan Montsià.",
        "en": "In Catalan mythology a marfanto is a kind of lost soul of the Terres de l'Ebre, specifically the Baix Ebre and Montsià."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "marfanto-c02",
      "source_id": "marfanto-s1",
      "quote": "els marfantos són unes criatures d'ultratomba afligides que es passegen pels aiguamolls a les nits sense lluna i fan uns esgarips que glacen la sang dels qui se'ls troben.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Para marfanto adalah makhluk alam kubur yang menderita, berkeliaran di rawa pada malam tanpa bulan dan menjerit hingga darah orang yang bertemu mereka membeku.",
        "en": "The marfantos are grieving beings from beyond the grave who roam the marshes on moonless nights, shrieking cries that chill the blood of those who meet them."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "marfanto-c03",
      "source_id": "marfanto-s1",
      "quote": "Els seus crits són una mena de personificació del vent de la zona.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Jeritan mereka semacam personifikasi angin di daerah itu.",
        "en": "Their cries are a kind of personification of the local wind."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "marfanto-c04",
      "source_id": "marfanto-s1",
      "quote": "De vegades s'ha postulat que els marfantos podrien ser un record llegendari d'antics esclaus, moriscos, furtius o contrabandistes que vagarejaven per la zona del delta de l'Ebre.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ada dugaan marfanto adalah kenangan legendaris akan budak, orang Morisco, buronan, atau penyelundup yang dahulu berkeliaran di delta Ebro.",
        "en": "It has sometimes been suggested that the marfantos are a legendary memory of former slaves, Moriscos, fugitives or smugglers who wandered the Ebro delta."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "marfanto-c05",
      "source_id": "marfanto-s1",
      "quote": "una mena d'espantamainades que es feien servir per a atemorir els xiquets sota l'amenaça que, si no es portaven bé, «Vindran les marfantes!»",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Marfanta, versi perempuannya, adalah momok untuk menakuti anak-anak dengan ancaman \"Para marfanta akan datang!\" bila mereka nakal.",
        "en": "The marfantes, the female form, were bogeys used to scare children with the threat \"The marfantes will come!\" if they misbehaved."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "marfanto-c06",
```

```json
      "en": "The maria da manta is a horned monster with fire in its eyes that lives in rivers, lakes and wells, luring and drowning children who come near. She belongs to the Medos or Papões used by parents to frighten children, and a folk rhyme says she kills children in pairs.",

      "quote": "Ela surge como uma entidade malévola e assustadora do sono, um monstro com cornos e lume nos olhos que mora nos rios lagos e poços.",

        "en": "The maria da manta is an evil, frightening being, a horned monster with fire in its eyes that lives in rivers, lakes and wells."

      "source_id": "maria-da-manta-s1",
      "quote": "Segundo as lendas, ela atrai as crianças que se aproximam destes lugares aquáticos e afoga-as.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut legenda, ia memikat anak-anak yang mendekati perairan itu lalu menenggelamkan mereka.",
        "en": "In legend she lures children who come near these waters and drowns them."
      }
    },

      "id": "maria-da-manta-c03",
      "source_id": "maria-da-manta-s1",
      "quote": "Enquadra-se num conjunto de criaturas chamado Medos ou Papões, por serem utilizados pelos pais e educadores para assustar as crianças",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia termasuk kelompok makhluk yang disebut Medos atau Papões, yang dipakai orang tua dan pendidik untuk menakuti anak-anak.",
        "en": "She belongs to a group of creatures called Medos or Papões, used by parents and teachers to frighten children."
      }
    },

      "source_id": "maria-da-manta-s1",
      "quote": "Leite de Vasconcelos diz que a Maria-da-Manta é uma \"evidente transformação de uma antiga divindade.\"",

      "statement": {

      "source_id": "maria-da-manta-s1",
      "quote": "Também conhecida como Marimanta em mirandês e na Galiza. Em Badajoz celebra-se a queima da Marimanta.",

      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {

      "source_id": "maria-da-manta-s1",
      "quote": "Tem lume nos olhos ... E lenha nos cornos ... Corre montes e vales ... E mata meninos aos pares.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Sebuah sajak rakyat menyebutnya bermata api, bertanduk kayu bakar, menjelajah gunung dan lembah, dan membunuh anak-anak berpasangan.",
        "en": "A folk rhyme says she has fire in her eyes and firewood on her horns, roams hills and valleys, and kills children in pairs."
```

```json
        "en": "In the legends of Mallorca she is called Maria Enganxa."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "maria-gancha-c04",
      "source_id": "maria-gancha-s1",
      "quote": "No caso da Maria Gancha, os poços eram os locais perigosos a evitar.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Sebagai salah satu Medos atau Papões penakut anak, Maria Gancha dipakai agar anak-anak menjauhi sumur.",
        "en": "As one of the child-scaring Medos or Papões, the Maria Gancha was used to keep children away from wells."
      }
    }
  ],
```

```json
  "short_description": {
    "id": "Marranghino adalah roh rumah jenaka dari Lucania, pendek, berkumis, dan buncit, yang suka menyembunyikan perkakas petani.",
    "en": "The Marranghino is a joking household spirit of Lucania, short, moustached and pot-bellied, that likes to hide farmers' tools.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "marranghino-c01",
      "marranghino-c04",
      "marranghino-c06"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Marranghino adalah tokoh cerita rakyat Lucania, terutama di provinsi Matera, yang mitosnya mirip Monachicchio dan kemungkinan berasal dari tradisi Lares dan Penates. Ia sangat pendek, berkumis, berperut buncit, dan berkepala besar.",
      "en": "The Marranghino is a figure of Lucanian folklore, especially in the province of Matera, whose myth resembles the Monachicchio and probably derives from the traditions of the Lares and Penates. It is very short, moustached, pot-bellied and big-headed.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "marranghino-c01",
        "marranghino-c03",
        "marranghino-c04"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Ia bukan makhluk jahat, melainkan roh jenaka yang suka bermain dengan orang tanpa merugikan, dan paling suka menyembunyikan perkakas petani. Dalam dialek beberapa desa, marranghino juga berarti sejenis laba-laba ramping.",
      "en": "It is not wicked but a joking spirit that plays with people harmlessly, and especially likes hiding farmers' tools. In some village dialects marranghino also means a kind of thin spider.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "marranghino-c02",
        "marranghino-c05",
        "marranghino-c06"
      ]
    }
  ],
  "cultural_context": null,
  "etymology": null,
```
