  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "marranghino-c01",
      "source_id": "marranghino-s1",
      "quote": "Il Marranghino è un personaggio immaginario del folclore lucano. Il suo mito condivide tratti comuni con quello del Monachicchio, ed è diffuso particolarmente nella provincia di Matera.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Marranghino adalah tokoh khayalan cerita rakyat Lucania yang mitosnya mirip Monachicchio dan tersebar terutama di provinsi Matera.",
        "en": "The Marranghino is an imaginary figure of Lucanian folklore whose myth shares traits with the Monachicchio, found especially in the province of Matera."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "marranghino-c02",
      "source_id": "marranghino-s1",
      "quote": "Nel dialetto di taluni paesi il termine marranghino si riferisce a un particolare tipo di ragno dalle zampe e corpo sottili.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam dialek beberapa desa, kata marranghino juga berarti sejenis laba-laba berkaki dan bertubuh ramping.",
        "en": "In some village dialects marranghino also means a kind of spider with thin legs and body."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "marranghino-c03",
      "source_id": "marranghino-s1",
      "quote": "Probabilmente anche il Marranghino nasce dalle tradizioni religiose pagane legate ai Lari e ai Penati.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Marranghino kemungkinan berasal dari tradisi religius pagan tentang Lares dan Penates.",
        "en": "The Marranghino probably derives from pagan religious traditions of the Lares and Penates."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "marranghino-c04",
      "source_id": "marranghino-s1",
      "quote": "il Marranghino si presenta con un aspetto goffo: particolarmente basso di statura, con i mustacchi, un grosso pancione e una grande testa.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia berpenampilan canggung: sangat pendek, berkumis, berperut buncit, dan berkepala besar.",
        "en": "It looks clumsy: very short, moustached, pot-bellied and big-headed."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "marranghino-c05",
      "source_id": "marranghino-s1",
      "quote": "Il Marranghino non viene descritto come un essere cattivo, ma piuttosto come uno spiritello burlone che si diverte a scherzare, ridere e giocare con le persone, senza causare danni.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia bukan makhluk jahat, melainkan roh kecil jenaka yang suka bercanda, tertawa, dan bermain dengan orang tanpa merugikan.",
        "en": "It is not wicked but a joking little spirit that likes to jest, laugh and play with people without causing harm."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "marranghino-c06",
      "source_id": "marranghino-s1",
      "quote": "Nelle leggende e nelle fiabe tradizionali, si diverte in modo particolare a nascondere gli attrezzi contadini durante il lavoro agricolo.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam legenda dan dongeng, ia paling suka menyembunyikan perkakas petani saat bekerja di ladang.",
        "en": "In legends and tales it especially enjoys hiding farmers' tools during field work."
      }
    }
  ],

  ],
  "short_description": {
    "id": "Marròca adalah hewan mitos menjijikkan dari kepercayaan petani, persilangan siput dan ular air, yang menghuni kolam dan selokan.",
    "en": "The marròca is a repulsive mythical animal of peasant belief, a cross between a snail and a water snake, haunting pools and sewers.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "marroca-c01",
      "marroca-c02"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Marròca adalah hewan mitologis yang menurut kepercayaan petani hidup di daerah basah pedesaan. Ia persilangan siput besar dan ular air, kadang tak berbentuk, tinggal di kolam tergenang dan selokan, dan bersuara serak di dekat pusaran air sehingga anak-anak menjauhi tempat berlumpur.",
      "en": "The marròca is a mythological animal that peasant belief places in the countryside wetlands. It is a cross between a big snail and a water snake, sometimes shapeless, living in stagnant pools and sewers and making a hoarse sound near eddies, so children keep away from muddy places.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "marroca-c01",
        "marroca-c02",
        "marroca-c03"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Di Viterbo, khususnya kaldera Latera, marròca berarti penyihir atau perempuan jahat, sedangkan di Fastello ia mirip gurita yang meracuni air. Hewan serupa di Caltanissetta disebut biddrina.",
      "en": "In Viterbo, especially the Latera caldera, the marròca means a witch or wicked woman, while in Fastello it is an octopus-like creature that poisons water. A similar animal in Caltanissetta is called the biddrina.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "marroca-c04",
        "marroca-c05",
        "marroca-c06"
      ]
    }
  ],
  "cultural_context": null,

    {

      "publisher": "Wikipedia (it)",
      "published": null,
      "language": "it",

      "accessed": "2026-10-02"
    }
  ],
  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "marroca-c01",
      "source_id": "marroca-s1",
      "quote": "La marròca è un animale mitologico che secondo la credenza contadina vive essenzialmente nelle zone umide della campagna.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Marròca adalah hewan mitologis yang menurut kepercayaan petani hidup terutama di daerah basah pedesaan.",
        "en": "The marròca is a mythological animal that, in peasant belief, lives mainly in the wetlands of the countryside."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "marroca-c02",
      "source_id": "marroca-s1",
      "quote": "Si tratterebbe di un animale ributtante, un incrocio tra una grossa lumaca ed una biscia (un serpente d'acqua), e talvolta di forma indefinita, che ama vivere nelle pozze di acqua stagnante, e nelle fogne.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia hewan menjijikkan, persilangan siput besar dan ular air, kadang tak berbentuk jelas, yang suka tinggal di kolam air tergenang dan selokan.",
        "en": "It is a repulsive animal, a cross between a big snail and a water snake, sometimes shapeless, that likes stagnant pools and sewers."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "marroca-c03",
      "source_id": "marroca-s1",
      "quote": "Emetterebbe un suono rauco specialmente in presenza di mulinelli, ed i bambini spaventati si tengono alla larga da tutte le falde melmose in cui potrebbe esserci una marroca.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia konon bersuara serak terutama di dekat pusaran air, sehingga anak-anak yang takut menjauhi semua tempat berlumpur yang mungkin dihuni marròca.",
        "en": "It supposedly makes a hoarse sound, especially near eddies, so frightened children keep away from any muddy place where a marròca might be."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "marroca-c04",
      "source_id": "marroca-s1",
      "quote": "In provincia di Viterbo, nell'alto Lazio, ed in particolare nella caldera dell'ex vulcano di Latera, si ritrova la marroca con un'accezione differente: simboleggerebbe una strega o comunque una donna brutta e malvagia.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Di provinsi Viterbo, Lazio utara, khususnya kaldera gunung api Latera, marròca berarti lain: penyihir atau perempuan jelek dan jahat.",
        "en": "In the province of Viterbo in northern Lazio, especially the caldera of the former Latera volcano, the marròca means something else: a witch or an ugly, wicked woman."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "marroca-c05",
      "source_id": "marroca-s1",
      "quote": "viene fatta assomigliare ad una specie di piovra e ha la capacità di rendere l’acqua velenosa per chi la beve.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Di Fastello ia dibayangkan mirip gurita dan dapat membuat air beracun bagi peminumnya.",
        "en": "In Fastello it is imagined like an octopus and can make water poisonous to whoever drinks it."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "marroca-c06",
      "source_id": "marroca-s1",
      "quote": "Animale simile ma con nome diverso è la biddrina, che infesterebbe le zone umide della campagna della provincia di Caltanissetta",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Hewan serupa dengan nama lain adalah biddrina, yang konon menghuni daerah basah di provinsi Caltanissetta.",
        "en": "A similar animal with a different name is the biddrina, said to haunt the wetlands of the province of Caltanissetta."
      }
    }
  ],

    {
      "id": "Para Maruxinho adalah makhluk gaib cerita rakyat Portugal utara di Chaves, semacam duende yang tinggal di reruntuhan castro atau gua bawah tanah di bawah desa. Mereka kecil dan kurus, berwajah keriput, bertelinga besar, dan bercakar. Mereka cerdik dan licik serta dapat bersembunyi di celah mana pun, sehingga orang cerdik masih disebut \"fino como um maruxinho\".",
      "en": "The Maruxinhos are enchanted beings of the folklore of northern Portugal in Chaves, a kind of duende living in ruined hillforts or underground caves beneath the villages. They are small and slight, with wrinkled faces, big ears and claws. They are clever and cunning and can hide in any crack, so a sly person is still called \"fino como um maruxinho\".",

    {
      "id": "maruxinho-s1",

      "title": "Maruxinho",

      "publisher": "Wikipedia (pt)",
      "published": null,
      "language": "pt",
      "type": "wikipedia",
      "accessed": "2026-10-02"
    }
  ],
  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "maruxinho-c01",
      "source_id": "maruxinho-s1",
      "quote": "Os Maruxinhos  são seres encantados do folclore do norte de Portugal no concelho de Chaves.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Para Maruxinho adalah makhluk gaib cerita rakyat Portugal utara di kotamadya Chaves.",
        "en": "The Maruxinhos are enchanted beings of the folklore of northern Portugal in the municipality of Chaves."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "maruxinho-c02",
      "source_id": "maruxinho-s1",
      "quote": "São uma espécie de duendes que vivem nas ruínas de velhos castros ou nas grutas subterrâneas que passam por baixo das aldeias.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Mereka semacam duende yang tinggal di reruntuhan benteng kuno (castro) atau gua bawah tanah di bawah desa-desa.",
        "en": "They are a kind of duende living in the ruins of old hillforts (castros) or in underground caves running beneath the villages."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "maruxinho-c03",
      "source_id": "maruxinho-s1",
      "quote": "Os maruxinhos  são de pequena estatura, franzinos, tem o rosto enrugado e os olhos vivos, são orelhudos e possuem garras em vez de mãos.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Mereka bertubuh kecil dan kurus, berwajah keriput dengan mata hidup, bertelinga besar, dan bercakar alih-alih bertangan.",
        "en": "They are small and slight, with wrinkled faces and lively eyes, big ears, and claws instead of hands."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "maruxinho-c04",
      "source_id": "maruxinho-s1",
      "quote": "Os Maruxinhos são considerados seres espertos e matreiros que escondem-se em qualquer fresta.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Mereka dianggap cerdik dan licik, dan dapat bersembunyi di celah mana pun.",
        "en": "They are considered clever and cunning, able to hide in any crack."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "maruxinho-c05",
      "source_id": "maruxinho-s1",
      "quote": "Ainda é usada a expressão \"És fino como um maruxinho!\". para dizer que uma pessoa é esperta e matreira",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ungkapan \"És fino como um maruxinho!\" (\"Kau cerdik seperti maruxinho!\") masih dipakai untuk orang yang cerdik dan licik.",
        "en": "The saying \"És fino como um maruxinho!\" (\"You're as sly as a maruxinho!\") is still used of a clever, cunning person."
      }
    }
  ],
  "conflicts": [],

Di seluruh Algarve orang percaya Medos muncul pada tengah hari, tengah malam, atau setelah lonceng Ave Maria, terutama pada musim panas di malam terang bulan dan tengah hari terik ketika semua orang beristirahat.",

      "quote": "É sobretudo no verão que os Medos aparecem, nas belas noites de luar e ao pino do meio dia, pela força do calor, quando tudo dorme ou descansa .",

      "context": "traditional-belief",

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Mekhala adalah penyihir jahat dari La Paz, Bolivia, yang melambangkan kekeringan, embun beku, dan kelaparan, dan mencuri hasil ladang.",
    "en": "The mekhala is an evil witch of La Paz, Bolivia, representing drought, frost and famine, who steals the harvest.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "mekhala-c01",
      "mekhala-c06"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Mekhala atau miqala adalah makhluk jahat dari departemen La Paz, Bolivia, yang melambangkan kekeringan, embun beku, dan kelaparan. Dalam kisah yang dihimpun Alison Spedding di Yungas, ia penyihir yang terbang telanjang pada malam hari setelah mencopot matanya dan menaruh lilin menyala di rongga matanya. Orang yang bertemu dengannya harus melemparkan sandal kirinya.",
      "en": "The mekhala or miqala is an evil being of the department of La Paz, Bolivia, representing droughts, frosts and famine. In the tales gathered by Alison Spedding in the Yungas, she is a witch who flies naked at night after removing her eyes and putting lit candles in the sockets. Anyone who meets her must throw their left sandal at her.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "mekhala-c01",
        "mekhala-c02",
        "mekhala-c03"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Menurut Rigoberto Paredes, mekhala adalah dewi jahat bertubuh perempuan yang tinggi dan kurus, berperut menjuntai sampai lutut, dan berekor api seperti komet. Jubah merahnya penuh kantong berisi makanan curian dari ladang, dan di kantong itu ia memenjarakan jiwa anak-anak yang tidur; ia juga mengisap darah hewan.",
      "en": "According to Rigoberto Paredes, mekhalas are evil deities with tall, thin women's bodies, bellies hanging to their knees and fiery tails like comets. Their red tunics are full of pockets holding food stolen from the fields, where they also imprison the souls of sleeping children; they also suck animals' blood.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "mekhala-c04",
        "mekhala-c05",
        "mekhala-c06",
        "mekhala-c07"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Orang melindungi diri dengan memanggil khonopa, dewa pelindung rumah, atau memasang arca Mama Sara. José Camarlinghi menulis tentang mekhala dalam puisinya Illa tocanta.",
      "en": "People protect themselves by invoking the khonopas, household guardian gods, or setting up an image of Mama Sara. José Camarlinghi wrote about the mekhalas in his poem Illa tocanta.",

        "mekhala-c08",
        "mekhala-c09"
      ]

        "en": "The mekhala or miqala is an evil mythological being of the department of La Paz, Bolivia, said to represent droughts, frosts and famine."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mekhala-c02",
      "source_id": "mekhala-s1",
      "quote": "Según estos relatos, este ser es una bruja que vuela desnuda por las noches y que se saca los ojos y los guarda en una olla de barro, colocando una vela encendida en las órbitas vacías",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut kisah yang dihimpun Alison Spedding di Yungas, ia penyihir yang terbang telanjang pada malam hari, mencopot matanya dan menyimpannya di periuk tanah liat, lalu menaruh lilin menyala di rongga matanya.",
        "en": "In the tales gathered by Alison Spedding in the Yungas, she is a witch who flies naked at night, removes her eyes and keeps them in a clay pot, putting a lit candle in the empty sockets."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mekhala-c03",
      "source_id": "mekhala-s1",
      "quote": "Para protegerse, las personas que se crucen con ella deben lanzarle su abarca izquierda, pero sobre todo estar atentas, ya que las mekhalas tienen los ojos rojos y las cuencas lastimadas.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Untuk melindungi diri, orang yang berpapasan dengannya harus melemparkan sandal kirinya, dan waspada karena mekhala bermata merah dengan rongga mata terluka.",
        "en": "To protect themselves, people who meet her must throw their left sandal at her and stay alert, since mekhalas have red eyes and wounded sockets."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mekhala-c04",
      "source_id": "mekhala-s1",
      "quote": "Según las versiones recopiladas por el etnógrafo boliviano Rigoberto Paredes, las mekhalas son deidades maléficas con cuerpo de mujer, altas, flacas y con el cabello despeinado y suelto.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut versi yang dihimpun etnograf Rigoberto Paredes, mekhala adalah dewi jahat bertubuh perempuan, tinggi, kurus, dengan rambut kusut terurai.",
        "en": "In the versions gathered by the ethnographer Rigoberto Paredes, mekhalas are evil deities with women's bodies, tall, thin and with loose, tangled hair."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mekhala-c05",
      "source_id": "mekhala-s1",
      "quote": "Se dice que tienen una barriga que les cae hasta las rodillas y una cola de fuego «como un cometa»",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Perut mereka konon menjuntai sampai lutut dan mereka berekor api seperti komet.",
        "en": "They are said to have bellies hanging to their knees and a fiery tail like a comet."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mekhala-c06",
      "source_id": "mekhala-s1",
      "quote": "Lleva una larga túnica de color rojo cubierta de varios bolsillos, los cuales llena de alimentos que roba de sembradíos y huertos.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia memakai jubah merah panjang penuh kantong yang diisinya dengan makanan curian dari ladang dan kebun.",
        "en": "She wears a long red tunic covered in pockets, which she fills with food stolen from fields and gardens."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "mekhala-c07",
      "source_id": "mekhala-s1",
      "quote": "se dice que en estos bolsillos las mekhalas aprisionan las almas de niños dormidos que se cruzan en su camino y que si se topan con un animal le chupan la sangre, cual vampiros.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Di kantong itu mekhala memenjarakan jiwa anak-anak yang tidur, dan bila bertemu hewan mereka mengisap darahnya seperti vampir.",
        "en": "In these pockets mekhalas imprison the souls of sleeping children, and if they meet an animal they suck its blood like vampires."

    {
      "id": "mekhala-c08",
      "source_id": "mekhala-s1",
      "quote": "Para defenderse, hay que invocar a khonopas o qonopas, dioses menores protectores de las casas o poner una imagen de Mama Sara, una waka o ídolo del maíz",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Untuk melawannya orang memanggil khonopa, dewa kecil pelindung rumah, atau memasang arca Mama Sara, waka atau berhala jagung.",
        "en": "To defend oneself one invokes the khonopas, minor household guardian gods, or sets up an image of Mama Sara, a waka or maize idol."
